# Tetembungan lan pilihan terjemahan

Indeks iki ngemot tetembungan Inggris lan padanané ing basa Jawa kanggo wacan OpenLogic. Katrangan basa ndhukung ejaan, ragam, utawa panganggoné tembung manut bukti kang kacathet. Kolom panggonan nuduhaké tuladha pasangan kang ditemokaké ing sumber Inggris lan sumber Jawa.

## logic → logika

Direct scholarly and official orthographic evidence.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0031, larik 80](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/pairing.tex#L80).

## formal logic → logika formal

Phrase directly observed; English definitions govern technical details.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0001, larik 7](../translation/content/open-logic-about.tex#L7).

## set; naive set theory → himpunan; teori himpunan naif

No qualifying Javanese set-theory witness was found. Kumpulan has only ordinary gathering senses in the checked dictionary and is retained as negative evidence. The official borrowing rule, government-edited Javanese mathematics use of international terms, and direct scholarly use of teori support the borrowing pattern, while each mathematical sense is defined from OpenLogic rather than Indonesian. The complete term remains provisional and reversible.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0003, larik 11](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets-functions-relations-complete.tex#L11).

## element/member → anggota; unsur (definitional synonym)

General member lexeme attested; formal membership defined directly from OLP-0005. Token element renders anggota for both number forms; introductory synonym unsur is a declared borrowing.

## extensionality → ekstensionalitas

The international technical stem remains a controlled borrowing. OLP-0005 defines the exact biconditional, and OLP-0013 uses extensionality only to show that two unequal candidate sets cannot both be identical to one order relation; no canon passage is claimed to attest the set-theoretic sense.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0005, larik 10](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/basics.tex#L10).

## subset → subhimpunan

Subhimpunan remains a transparent controlled compound under the documented borrowing practice, but no inspected canon source directly attests it. OLP-0006 defines it exactly by element inclusion and its notation.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0006, larik 62](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/subsets.tex#L62).

## proper subset → subhimpunan sejati

The term is retained only as a source-defined specialization: OLP-0006 requires both subset inclusion and inequality. No canon passage is claimed to establish sejati as a mathematical properness marker.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0006, larik 25](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/subsets.tex#L25).

## power set → himpunan pangkat

The controlled calque is defined in OLP-0006 as the set of all subsets. JV-P018 is only a negative control for the numerical power sense of rambang; no inspected canon passage attests the full set-theory term.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0010, larik 30](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/russells-paradox.tex#L30).

## if and only if → yen lan mung yen

The explicit composition preserves both implications and is never collapsed to yen. General academic conditional and definitional syntax is attested, but the formal connective phrase remains controlled by the English definitions and formulas.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0012, larik 57](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/relations-as-sets.tex#L57).

## empty → kosong

Empty set has no members; empty string has length zero. Keep distinctions in definitions.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0005, larik 16](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/basics.tex#L16).

## proof / prove → bukti / buktekna

General bukti homonym 2 and verbal form attested. Formal mathematical argument sense derives from source. Unaccented e follows edition policy.

## true / false → bener / salah

Bebener and contrasting kleru provide general truth-vocabulary evidence; use bener and salah consistently in formal contexts. No formal truth-value canon attestation claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0452, larik 32](../translation/content/normal-modal-logic/filtrations/preliminaries.tex#L32).

## natural number → wilangan natural

Wilangan is directly attested and the borrowing strategy is documented, but the complete mathematical phrase is not canon-attested. The OLP-0007 display explicitly includes 0, preventing any silent positive-only convention.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0021, larik 146](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/functions/function-basics.tex#L146).

## integer → wilangan wutuh

Wilangan is directly attested; wutuh has its ordinary whole sense. Their mathematical compound remains source-defined by the OLP-0007 integer display, including negatives and zero.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0021, larik 84](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/functions/function-basics.tex#L84).

## rational / real number → wilangan rasional / wilangan real

The recognizable international stems follow the bounded borrowing policy and occur alongside an attested number headword. OLP-0007 fixes their basic scope. OLP-0043 constructs each rational as an equivalence class of integer pairs with nonzero second coordinate, defines arithmetic and order on representatives, and embeds each integer as the class of its pair with 1. These exact sets and operations come from the source definitions and formulas rather than the canon; no native Javanese foundations attestation is claimed.

## finite / infinite / infinity / actual infinity → winates / tanpa wates / bab tanpa wates / tanpa wates kang nyata

Kang tanpa wates is directly attested only for general boundlessness, and the academic passages support prose register rather than foundations terminology. Finite-cardinal, infinite-sequence, infinity, and especially medieval actual-infinity senses remain defined by the exact English source, examples, and formulas. Tanpa wates kang nyata is retained as a high-uncertainty historical descriptor, not a claimed conventional term, and remains open to expert replacement if a Javanese higher-mathematics witness establishes one.

## string / sequence → rerangken / urutan

OLP-0007 defines a finite string as a finite sequence over an alphabet and separately defines one-way infinite sequences. General academic ordering and boundlessness language is supported, while the computer-science distinction and rerangken term remain source-defined and provisional.

## union / intersection → gabungan / irisan

The terms remain controlled regional borrowings rather than canon-attested Javanese set terminology. OLP-0008 defines union with inclusive OR and intersection with AND, including binary, family, and indexed forms; the formulas control their exact meanings.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0010, larik 94](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/russells-paradox.tex#L94).

## set difference / disjoint → selisih himpunan / pisah

OLP-0008 defines directional A minus B as A-members not in B and defines disjoint by empty intersection. Neither full term is claimed as directly canon-attested.

## ordered pair / tuple / Cartesian product → pasangan urut / tupel / produk Kartesius

OLP-0009 defines ordered pairs by Wiener--Kuratowski, left-associates longer tuples, and defines the Cartesian product as all pairs with first coordinate in A and second in B. The international terms follow bounded borrowing practice but are not claimed as canon-attested Javanese mathematics terminology.

## paradox / naive set theory → paradoks / teori himpunan naif

Teori is attested and the borrowing policy is documented, while the full mathematical compounds remain source-defined. OLP-0010 supplies Russell's contradiction and explains genuinely naive as lacking precise existence axioms, not as an insult or a claim of careless mathematics.

## perfect number / proper divisor → wilangan sampurna / wilangan kang mbagi tanpa turah nanging dudu wilangan iku dhewe

Perfect number remains a controlled source-defined specialization. The government-edited arithmetic witness attests dipara and ora turah, while the exact mathematical condition remains controlled by OLP-0005.

## relation / binary relation / bears a relation → relasi / relasi biner / relasi lumaku antarane objek

General relasi is directly observed in Javanese scholarly prose; OLP-0012 defines the binary mathematical sense as a subset of A squared. Ambiguous passive diduweni renderings were replaced by explicit holds-between language or, for relations viewed as sets, contains-pairs language so argument roles cannot reverse.

## reflexive / irreflexive / transitive → refleksif / irrefleksif / transitif

No inspected canon passage attests the formal relation senses. OLP-0014 defines them by exact quantified conditions: reflexive requires every diagonal pair, irreflexive excludes every diagonal pair, and transitive requires Rxz whenever Rxy and Ryz.

## symmetric / anti-symmetric / asymmetric → simetris / antisimetris / asimetris

The three borrowings name three distinct source-defined quantified conditions in OLP-0014. Symmetry reverses every related pair; antisymmetry permits both directions only on the diagonal and may coexist with symmetry; asymmetry forbids both directions for every pair.

## connected / order / strict order → koneks / tatanan / tatanan ketat

OLP-0014 defines a connected relation by comparability of every pair of distinct elements; reserve kasambungan graf for graph connectivity. OLP-0016 defines the complete order hierarchy and the strict-order conditions. Tatanan and koneks remain source-defined editorial specializations; the inspected canon does not attest them as order-theory terminology.

## identity relation / universal relation / empty relation → relasi identitas / relasi universal / relasi kosong

OLP-0012 defines the identity relation by the diagonal, the universal relation by A squared, and the empty relation by the empty set. Their explanations now say that the set representation contains every or no ordered pair. General relation vocabulary is attested, while the three formal compounds remain provisional.

## comprehension / contradiction / axiom → komprehensi / kontradiksi / aksioma

The terms follow the bounded technical-borrowing strategy but are not directly attested in a Javanese foundations canon. OLP-0010 fixes the comprehension, Russell-contradiction and axiom senses. The source proofs and formulas control the logical sense.

## theorem / proposition / definition / recursive → teorema / proposisi / definisi / rekursif

The international technical stems remain controlled borrowings within attested academic prose architecture. Environment labels retain source hierarchy; in OLP-0013, proposisi means the content expressed by a predicate-bearing sentence and differs from a numbered proposition environment. The exact technical senses remain source-defined. OLP-0091 ties teorema and each propositional heading to exact natural-deduction conditions, so the adapted labels remain source-defined rather than ordinary claims of truth. OLP-0105 defines teorema through a closed tableau for false A and fixes each proposition heading by its exact tableau-metatheoretic formula.

## equivalence relation / equivalence class / quotient / partition → relasi ekuivalensi / kelas ekuivalensi / kuosien / partisi

OLP-0015 defines an equivalence relation by reflexivity, symmetry, and transitivity; its equivalence class is exactly the set of related elements and its quotient is the set of all such classes. OLP-0042 applies that construction to integer differences, and OLP-0043 applies it to integer fractions by identifying pairs exactly when their cross-products agree. General relation and academic classification prose are attested, but the four mathematical compounds and these quotient constructions remain source-defined provisional borrowings.

## preorder / partial order / total or linear order → pratatanan / tatanan parsial / tatanan total utawa linear

OLP-0016 preserves the exact hierarchy: reflexive plus transitive gives a preorder; adding antisymmetry gives a partial order; adding connectedness gives a total or linear order. Strict forms remain separately marked. Pra- plus tatanan is an explicit editorial coinage, not alleged established terminology.

## reflexive closure / extension / initial segment → tutupan refleksif / ekstensi / perangan wiwitan

OLP-0016 directly defines sequence extension and initial segment, and defines reflexive closure by adding the diagonal to a strict order. The converse removes the diagonal. OLP-0045 consistently uses perangan wiwitan sejati for a proper initial segment of the rationals and immediately fixes it by the strict-subset formula and downward-closure condition. The operations and formulas control these specialist meanings; no inspected canon passage attests them as order-theory terminology.

## graph / directed graph / vertex / edge → graf / graf mawa arah / simpul / rusuk

OLP-0017 defines a directed graph as a vertex set together with an edge relation contained in its square. The carrier is explicit, so isolated vertices distinguish graphs that share the same edge relation. General relation and academic prose are attested, while the graph-theory terms remain source-defined provisional borrowings.

## tree / root / ancestor / child / parent / branch / subtree → wit / oyod / leluhur / anak / induk / pang / subwit

OLP-0018 defines every graph- and order-theoretic role and controls the specialist senses. The canon supports borrowing policy, relation vocabulary, and continuous scholarly definition prose, but it does not attest this tree-theory family; the native metaphors therefore remain explicit, reversible source-defined specializations.

## least element / well-ordered / successor / predecessor / chain → anggota paling cilik / katata kanthi becik / panerus / pandhisik / rantai

The exact OLP-0018 definitions control: least compares with every carrier element, well-order quantifies over each nonempty subset, successor and predecessor are immediate cover roles, and a branch is a maximal chain. Anggota is lexically attested and the canon supports relation and scholarly-definition prose, but no passage attests the complete order-theory compounds.

## inverse / relative product / restriction / application / transitive closure → invers / produk relatif / watesan / aplikasi / tutupan transitif

OLP-0019 fixes every operation by formula: inverse swaps coordinates; relative product composes R then S through an existential middle element; restriction intersects R with A squared; application collects related outputs; transitive closure unions positive powers; reflexive-transitive closure adds the identity. The canon supports borrowing policy, anggota, relasi, and scholarly definition prose but not these specialist operation senses. R+ is section-local and differs from its reflexive-closure use in OLP-0016, as the reader errata states.

## finitely branching → nyabang winates

OLP-0018 immediately defines nyabang winates as every node having finitely many successors. That local definition prevents confusion with finite branch length or finitely many total branches. Canon supports borrowing policy, academic definition syntax, and the ordinary boundlessness phrase, but does not attest the coined tree-theory term; no attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0018, larik 109](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/trees.tex#L109).

## formula / derivation / completeness / first-order logic → formula / derivasi / kelengkapan / logika tataran kapisan

Formula, derivasi, and kelengkapan remain controlled technical adaptations; tataran kapisan is a descriptive choice, and derivasi stays distinct from bukti. The canon attests logika and logika formal, borrowing policy, and sustained technical prose, but no preserved passage attests the complete logic-foundations family used in OLP-0018-P005; its exact source-defined use therefore remains provisional. OLP-0091 uses derivasi for the finite natural-deduction witness and kelengkapan for the theorem paired with soundness; every occurrence is fixed by the adjoining definition or correspondence claim. OLP-0092 uses derivasi for each named natural-deduction witness and kelengkapan for the theorem whose later proof uses every stated property. OLP-0093 uses derivasi for six exact natural-deduction trees and kelengkapan for the later theorem that needs these connective facts. OLP-0094 uses derivasi for the named strong-generalization witness and kelengkapan for the theorem requiring the quantified facts. OLP-0095 uses derivasi throughout the inference-count induction and its two closing soundness corollaries. OLP-0096 uses derivasi for the identity-rule examples, the backward uniqueness construction and both final problem targets. OLP-0097 uses derivasi for the final identity-elimination case whose two premise derivations support the soundness transfer. OLP-0105 retains kelengkapan for the theorem paired with soundness in the correspondence between semantic and tableau proof-theoretic notions. OLP-0106 retains kelengkapan for the later theorem whose proof uses the four tableau provability-consistency results established in this section. OLP-0107 uses kelengkapan for the later theorem that needs the exact conjunction, disjunction and conditional tableau facts established here. OLP-0108 uses kelengkapan for the theorem requiring the quantified tableau facts and retains each formula in the strong-generalization construction. OLP-0109 uses derivasi for the system whose tableau rules are shown to preserve branch satisfiability and yield the three soundness consequences. OLP-0110 preserves each formula schema in the reflexive and signed identity rules and in both quantified tableau exercises. OLP-0111 follows the identity formula schema through a chosen assignment, equal term values and both extensionality applications. OLP-0112 uses the established derivation token in both tagged axiomatic chapter titles while preserving the distinct FOL and PL file identities. OLP-0113 defines an axiomatic derivation as a finite formula sequence and tracks every formula index through its premise, axiom or inference-rule basis. OLP-0116 preserves every formula and derivation line in three worked axiomatic examples and the concatenation proof of the conditional-chain proposition. OLP-0117 follows every formula through the quantified axiomatic derivation from two universal conjuncts to the universal closure of their pointwise conjunction. OLP-0118 follows every finite formula sequence through the axiomatic definitions and the concatenation and finite-premise constructions.

## function / mapping → fungsi / pemetaan

Fungsi is directly attested only in a general purpose/use sense. OLP-0021 controls the mathematical sense by mapping every domain member to one specific codomain member and repeatedly requires one and only one value for each argument. Pemetaan is retained as a controlled technical borrowing within that definition; no formal-function attestation is inferred from the general canon.

## domain / codomain / range / argument / value / input / output → domain / kodomain / jangkauan / argumen / nilai / masukan / asil

OLP-0021 fixes the roles by definition and diagram: the domain supplies inputs or arguments, the codomain supplies possible values, and the range contains exactly attained values. Asil describes output and nilai the assigned value. General fungsi and academic prose are attested, but no passage attests this full mathematical vocabulary; codomain and attained range remain explicitly distinct.

## surjective / injective / bijective; surjection / injection / bijection → surjektif / injektif / bijektif; surjeksi / injeksi / bijeksi

OLP-0022 fixes the three senses by exact quantifiers: surjective requires at least one preimage for every codomain member, injective permits at most one, and bijective requires both. The worked constant, identity, successor, and parity functions preserve the distinctions. Source token keys and flags continue to select Javanese adjective and noun forms. Canon supports borrowing policy, general fungsi and academic prose, but no passage attests these mathematical terms or korespondensi siji-menyang-siji.

## function graph; functional; image → graf fungsi; fungsional; citra

The canon supports scholarly register, controlled borrowing, membership language, and general relasi/fungsi usage only; it does not independently attest these exact mathematical senses. OLP-0023 defines a graph as a subset of A times B, functional as exactly uniqueness plus totality, and image as the attained-value set. OLFUN-004 and OLFUN-005 remain explicitly disclosed critical source exceptions rather than evidence of source-target parity.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0023, larik 93](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/functions/functions-relations.tex#L93).

## inverse; left inverse; right inverse; composition → invers; invers kiwa; invers tengen; komposisi

The canon supports academic and proof register, controlled borrowing, and general relation/function vocabulary, but it does not attest these exact mathematical senses. OLP-0024 fixes left and right by the displayed cancellation equations, and OLP-0025 fixes composition order as first f then g. OLFUN-001 remains an explicitly disclosed critical source exception: the translated theorem uses the simple nonempty-domain correction and its adjacent note gives the exact condition A nonempty or B empty.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0019, larik 24](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/operations.tex#L24).

## partial function; total; defined; undefined; serial → fungsi parsial; total; ditetepake; ora ditetepake; serial

The canon supports scholarly prose, controlled borrowing, membership language, and general relasi/fungsi usage, but not these exact mathematical senses. OLP-0026 directly defines a partial function by at most one output, its actual domain by defined inputs, total by every ambient input, and seriality by existence of an output for each input. At most one remains distinct from exactly one.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0008, larik 16](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/unions-and-intersections.tex#L16).

## number; count; kind → wilangan; cacah; jinis

Freshly read recovered official KBJI originals confirm these lexical senses. They do not attest every mathematical compound. Preserve OpenLogic Nat including zero; old textbook wilangan asli excluding zero is not imported.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0003, larik 13](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets-functions-relations-complete.tex#L13).

## multiplication; division; divisibility without remainder; numerical sum; remainder → ping-pingan; para/paran; paran tanpa turah; gunggung; turah

Recovered dictionary HTML confirms the operation senses, while government-edited Javanese mathematics prose directly uses ping-pingan, dipara, and turah in calculations. OLP-0015 uses dipara and turah in modular arithmetic. OLP-0016 names divisibility provisionally as paran tanpa turah and defines n divides m by n mbagi m tanpa turah and m=kn; that specialist relation name is source-defined, not directly attested. Pamara means way of dividing, not divisor; total-function and union terminology are separate.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0021, larik 24](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/functions/function-basics.tex#L24).

## numerical power; power set; algebraic or logical term → rambang (numerical alternative); himpunan pangkat (retained); term (provisional technical borrowing)

Rambang directly attests equal-factor numerical powers, not powersets. The dictionary's suku entry lacks an algebraic or logical sense, so OLP-0055 and the formal-logic chapters use the international stem term as an explicitly provisional borrowing; exact formation rules, not the canon, control its technical meaning. Recovered rambang original HTML itself flattens its displayed example to 43; never use that as mathematical evidence. OLP-0101 uses term for both arbitrary closed instantiating terms and the substituted term in the exact quantifier-rule formulas. OLP-0104 chooses and reuses instantiating terms a, b and c in the exact quantified tableau branches while preserving every substitution.

## enumeration; enumerable; enumerability; countability → enumerasi; bisa didhaptar; enumerabilitas; bisa dietung (countable sense, source-defined)

The canon directly supports general number/count vocabulary and scholarly prose, not countable infinity or enumeration theory. OLP-0027 preserves the elementary list-or-surjection-from-positive-integers approach and the attributed bijection-with-Nat-or-an-initial-segment alternative. OLP-0029 controls the family by an informal list with every member at a finite position, a formal surjection from positive integers for nonempty sets, a separate empty-set case, the equivalent natural-number and finite-initial-segment formulations, and an explicit equation between the tokenized enumerable label and bisa didhaptar. OLP-0031 keeps enumerable unions distinct from finite unions and explicitly states the needed choice assumption for an arbitrary indexed family. OLP-0038 gives the set-theoretic alternative directly: an enumeration is a bijection whose domain is either all natural numbers or a finite initial segment, while the empty set is handled separately; the equivalent natural-number surjection and injection characterizations remain explicit. OLP-0039 proves non-enumerability by showing that every proposed Nat-indexed list omits a constructed element, for both infinite binary sequences and the powerset of Nat. OLP-0040 transfers a hypothetical powerset enumeration through a surjective characteristic-sequence map and thereby reduces enumeration of Bin^omega to enumeration of Pow(Nat). Avoidable active and passive verb forms built on enumerasi are replaced by direct Javanese listing language or menehi/diwenehi enumerasi tumrap. No computability requirement is introduced. The family remains high-uncertainty and reversible rather than conventionally attested.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0027, larik 45](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/size-of-sets-complete.tex#L45).

## ceiling function → fungsi pambunderan munggah

OLP-0029 and OLP-0038 both describe rounding upward to the nearest integer and preserve the ceiling brackets, alternating integer values, and even/odd piecewise definition. The OLP-0029 table also retains the audited missing-value correction. The government-edited mathematics passages support arithmetic register only; no specialized Javanese ceiling-function attestation or pronunciation certification is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0029, larik 141](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/enumerability.tex#L141).

## pairing function; encode; code; decode → fungsi pasangan; ngodeake; kode; ngudhari kode

OLP-0031 defines a pairing function broadly as an injection from A times B into Nat and defines a code as its value on a pair. Its final exercise now enumerates the range before applying the inverse and separately handles an empty product. OLP-0032 gives the bijective code h(n,m)=2^n(2m+1)-1 and separately the injective positive code j(n,m)=2^n3^m, explicitly noting that a nonsurjective encoding has only a partial inverse. These constructions control the coding direction; no native Javanese coding-theory attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0031, larik 55](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/pairing.tex#L55).

## cofinite; complement → kowinates; komplemen

Kowinates adapts the co- prefix to ko- plus the chosen winates form. OLP-0031 explicitly defines a subset of Nat as cofinite exactly when its complement within Nat is finite. No native Javanese topology or set-theory attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0031, larik 93](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/pairing.tex#L93).

## array; row; column; triangular number; numerical power → larikan rong dimensi; baris; kolom; wilangan segitiga; rambang

In OLP-0030, larikan rong dimensi remains an explicit high-uncertainty descriptive form fixed by the displayed grids, row and column coordinates and axis labels; no native array-theory attestation is claimed. OLP-0031 corrects the frozen strict triangular-number bound to an inclusive bound, matching the sequence 0, 1, 3, 6 and k(k+1)/2, while retaining wilangan segitiga as source-defined terminology. Gunggung supports numerical sum and rambang directly supports numerical powers; rambang is not transferred to powersets. English ordinal th in OLP-0031 is rendered by preceding kaping, with an empty text-only TeX exponent to preserve structure without residual English.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0030, larik 39](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/zig-zag.tex#L39).

## truth table; truth function → tabel kabeneran; fungsi kabeneran

General truth and function vocabulary does not attest these formal compounds. OLP-0031 defines the relevant truth functions as every function from a finite bit power to one bit for some finite arity, then asks for enumerability of their union. OLP-0061 defines recursive evaluation for every active connective, displays the corresponding truth tables, and asks for the table of one explicitly defined ternary connective. The formulas control every specialist sense; no native Javanese logic-textbook attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0031, larik 80](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/pairing.tex#L80).

## non-enumerable; diagonal method; diagonalization → ora bisa didhaptar; metodhe diagonal; diagonalisasi

OLP-0033 defines non-enumerability through the absence of a surjection from the positive integers and then proves it with two list-dependent diagonal constructions. JV-D015 now qualifies the surjection characterization to nonempty enumerable sets and explains beside the claim that the empty set is enumerable although no function from the positive integers to it exists. OLP-0039 repeats the two arguments under the Nat-bijection convention. In both units the constructed sequence or subset differs from every indexed proposal. Ora bisa didhaptar remains a descriptive rendering and the diagonal terms remain provisional borrowings; no native Javanese set-theory attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0033, larik 40](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/non-enumerability.tex#L40).

## reduction; reduce → reduksi; ngreduksi

OLP-0034 fixes the reduction direction operationally: an enumeration of A is transformed into one of B by a surjection A to B, so enumerating B reduces to enumerating A. OLP-0040 restates that direction under the alternate enumeration convention and instantiates it with the surjection sending each subset of Nat to its characteristic binary sequence. A hypothetical enumeration of Pow(Nat) would then enumerate all Bin^omega, contradicting the preceding theorem. Exercises preserve every source and target space. Avoidable ngenumerasi forms are replaced by menehi enumerasi tumrap or direct enumerasi tumrap constructions, while reduksi and ngreduksi remain adapted provisional borrowings under the orthographic policy. No effective or computable reduction condition is implied and no native Javanese computability-theory attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0034, larik 11](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/reduction.tex#L11).

## equinumerous; equinumerosity; cardinal size comparison → padha cacahe; gedhene himpunan; ora luwih gedhe tinimbang; luwih cilik tinimbang

General cacah and number vocabulary support only ordinary counting language. OLP-0035 defines padha cacahe exactly by a bijection and verifies the equivalence-relation laws. OLP-0036 defines ora luwih gedhe tinimbang by an injection and luwih cilik tinimbang by an injection with no bijection, with the needed Choice qualification on its unrestricted non-enumerability characterization. OLP-0037 states Schröder-Bernstein: opposite injection comparisons yield padha cacahe. OLP-0054 proves it by a closure-based sandwich lemma and two compositions. Its nested two-argument cardeq macro invocation expands to the valid chain A approximately B approximately C, so the central malformed-formula finding is rejected by the declared macro definition and complete proof. Gedhene Himpunan remains a high-uncertainty pedagogical heading, and no native attestation of transfinite cardinality is claimed.

## arithmetization → aritmetisasi

Aritmetisasi adapts the international technical stem under the checked borrowing policy. OLP-0041 fixes its chapter-local sense as the construction of multiple number systems within naive set theory, with ordered imports for integers, rationals, reals, cuts, reflections, detail checking and Cauchy sequences. It does not mean assigning Goedel numbers to syntax here. The canon supports academic prose, borrowing and general number vocabulary, but no native Javanese foundations text directly attests this specialist term.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0041, larik 8](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/arithmetization.tex#L8).

## construction of integers as equivalence classes; embedding natural numbers as integers → pambangunan wilangan wutuh minangka kelas ekuivalensi; nganggep wilangan natural minangka wilangan wutuh

OLP-0042 represents an integer difference a-b by the ordered pair (a,b), identifies pairs exactly when a+d=c+b, verifies reflexivity, symmetry and transitivity, and defines Int as the quotient of Nat squared by that relation. Addition, multiplication and order are then defined on equivalence classes. The embedding n_Int=[n,0] preserves addition, multiplication and order while maintaining the philosophical distinction that the constructed natural-number objects are not literally the integer equivalence classes. The canon supports academic prose, general member/relation/function/number language and arithmetic register, but no native Javanese foundations witness directly attests this quotient construction or canonical embedding.

## irrational number → wilangan irasional

Wilangan has a direct general number sense and irasional is an adapted international stem under the borrowing policy. OLP-0044 fixes the formal sense as a real number outside the rationals, then proves that square root of two has that status by geometric descent and the even-numerator/even-denominator contradiction. No specialized native Javanese attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0048, larik 23](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/cauchy.tex#L23).

## ordered field → lapangan katata

The existing ordering vocabulary combines with a provisional adapted field term. OLP-0044 says only that the rationals and reals are ordered fields and directs readers to the exact later definition; it does not silently supply or alter the field axioms. The canon supports borrowing and scholarly exposition but does not attest this mathematical compound.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0046, larik 19](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/reflections.tex#L19).

## Completeness Property; upper bound; lower bound; least upper bound; greatest lower bound → Sipat Kajangkepan; wates ndhuwur; wates ngisor; wates ndhuwur paling cilik; wates ngisor paling gedhe

OLP-0044 defines completeness compositionally and exactly: every nonempty set of reals that has an upper bound has a least upper bound. Its rational counterexample has upper bounds but no least rational upper bound. OLP-0045 uses the dual greatest-lower-bound phrase while motivating cuts. Capitalization marks the named property. The canon supports number vocabulary and academic definition prose, while ordinary jangkep does not itself attest this technical property.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0044, larik 83](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/reals.tex#L83).

## continuum; gap in the rational line → kontinuum; longkangan ing garis rasional

Kontinuum preserves the recognized technical stem, while longkangan retains OLP-0044's explicitly quoted spatial metaphor for the rationals' failure of completeness. The least-upper-bound example and later cut construction supply the mathematical content. No inspected canon passage is claimed to attest the full formal metaphor.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0007, larik 24](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/important-sets.tex#L24).

## cut; cuts (Dedekind construction) → potongan; potongan-potongan (pambangunan Dedekind)

The official dictionary directly records potongan with an ordinary cut or slice sense. OLP-0045 alone controls the formal Dedekind sense by three exact conditions: a nonempty proper initial segment of the rationals with no greatest member. Inclusion orders the cuts, and the union proof verifies all three conditions before proving leastness. The Indonesian dictionary definition is lexical scope evidence rather than a semantic pivot, and no native Javanese real-analysis attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0045, larik 41](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/cuts.tex#L41).

## embed the rationals into the reals → nyisipake wilangan rasional menyang wilangan real

OLP-0045 fixes the map exactly: rational p is represented by the cut of all rational q below p. Nyisipake is a transparent description of that structure-preserving representation rather than literal physical insertion; later verification controls preservation of the operations and order. No specialized native Javanese attestation is claimed.

## numbers or relations are sets, metaphysically speaking; treat them as sets; embed their theories → wilangan utawa relasi iku himpunan saka sisi metafisika; nganggep wilangan utawa relasi minangka himpunan; nyisipake teori-teorine

OLP-0013 distinguishes a metaphysical identity assertion from the weaker mathematical practice of treating certain relations as sets. OLP-0046 makes the parallel distinction for numbers: alternative ordered-pair implementations produce different set objects, natural zero is not literally the constructed integer zero, and the warranted conclusion is only that set theory lets us treat the numbers as sets and embed their theories. OLP-0053 adds that any Dedekind algebra exemplifies the desired natural-number structure and that all such algebras are isomorphic, while expressly withholding an answer about which objects are the natural numbers; the embedding is also conditional on a Dedekind-infinite set. General relasi, wilangan, konteks, and teori have canon support, while no Javanese canon source is claimed to settle the philosophy of mathematics.

## commutative ring; ordered ring → ring komutatif; ring katata

OLP-0047 fixes the two structures by formula: the commutative ring has eight displayed laws, and the ordered ring adds a total order compatible with addition and multiplication by nonnegative elements. Ring is retained as an internationally recognizable algebraic borrowing and katata follows the established ordering vocabulary. The canon supports formal-logic and scholarly definitional register plus general relation language, but it does not attest these algebraic compounds as conventional Javanese.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0047, larik 20](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/checking-details.tex#L20).

## associativity; commutativity; identity; additive inverse; distributivity; multiplicative inverse → asosiativitas; komutativitas; identitas; invers aditif; distributivitas; invers multiplikatif

OLP-0047 places these six adapted labels beside the exact ring and field formulas, so each label's algebraic role is fixed without relying on lexical resemblance. The canon supports controlled borrowing and scholarly exposition but does not directly attest the specialized algebraic labels.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0012, larik 119](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/relations-as-sets.tex#L119).

## trichotomy (connectedness of a total order) → trikotomi (koneksitas tatanan total)

OLP-0047 explicitly glosses trichotomy as connectedness for a total order and retains the three exclusive comparison cases in the adjacent formula. Trikotomi is an adapted technical borrowing and koneksitas reuses the established relation vocabulary. The canon supports formal and relation discourse but does not attest the full order-theoretic name.

## Cauchy sequence; tends to zero; limit → rerangken Cauchy; nyedhaki nol; limit

OLP-0048 defines a Cauchy sequence as a function from Nat to Rat whose values become pairwise epsilon-close beyond some natural bound, and defines tending to zero by the corresponding absolute-value condition. Equivalence is then fixed by the difference tending to zero. Rerangken is the established sequence word, nyedhaki gives the convergence description, and limit is a controlled international borrowing. The canon supports orthography, general function and number language and scholarly register, but no specialized native analysis attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0046, larik 29](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/arithmetization/reflections.tex#L29).

## decimal expansion; rational approximation → ekspansi desimal; panyedhakan rasional

OLP-0048 displays successive decimal prefixes of square root of two and states the exact error target 1/10^n, so the mathematical relation between decimal expansion and rational approximation is explicit. Ekspansi and desimal are controlled adaptations, while panyedhakan transparently describes approximation. The canon supports borrowing, general number language, arithmetic notation and academic prose but does not directly attest these real-analysis terms.

## well-defined; representative function → ditetepake kanthi becik; fungsi wakil

OLP-0048 immediately explains well-defined as independence from the chosen function in an equivalence class. Fungsi wakil denotes that chosen sequence-function, while the real object remains the entire equivalence class. The canon supports general member, relation and function vocabulary and explanatory definition prose, but it does not attest the quotient-construction compound as conventional terminology.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0036, larik 89](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/size-of-sets/comparing-size.tex#L89).

## monotonically decreasing; increasing → mudhun kanthi monoton; munggah kanthi monoton

The OLP-0048 bisection formulas keep f at or below its previous value and g at or above its previous value, fixing the two directions used in the completeness proof. Mudhun and munggah state those directions, while monoton is a controlled adaptation. The canon supports borrowing and scholarly prose but does not directly attest monotone-sequence terminology.

## Dedekind infinite; infinite set; infinitely many → tanpa wates miturut Dedekind; himpunan tanpa wates; tanpa wates cacahe

OLP-0049 introduces infinite sets as the chapter topic; the following units control the technical senses through Hilbert's Hotel examples, Dedekind's injection into a proper subset and cardinal comparisons. Tanpa wates has direct canon support only for general boundlessness, while himpunan and cacah have general lexical support. The exact source definitions and formulas therefore govern the foundations meanings, and no native Javanese attestation of Dedekind infinity is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0018, larik 105](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/trees.tex#L105).

## closure; closed under the successor function → tutupan; katutup marang fungsi panerus

OLP-0051 defines X as f-closed exactly when every f(x) for x in X remains in X, and defines the closure of o as the intersection of all such sets containing o. The lemma proves containment of o, closure and leastness; JV-D063 makes explicit its self-map scope. OLP-0054 generalizes from one generator to a subset and uses that closure in the Schröder-Bernstein sandwich proof; JV-D064 states the corresponding self-map and carrier-subset scope needed by the generalized definition and lemma. Tutupan and katutup transparently distinguish the generated object from the preservation property; the canon supports general function, member and scholarly-definition language but does not attest this specialist closure terminology.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0016, larik 121](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/relations/orders.tex#L121).

## Dedekind algebra; successor function; arithmetical induction → aljabar Dedekind; fungsi panerus; induksi aritmetis

OLP-0051 fixes a Dedekind algebra by the three exact conditions o outside the range of f, injectivity of f, and A equal to the f-closure of o. Its successor discussion fixes zero, injectivity and repeated generation. OLP-0052 supplies the induction theorem. Aljabar and aritmetis are controlled adaptations, fungsi has general canon support, and panerus is compositionally transparent; no native Javanese foundations source directly attests the complete compounds.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0022, larik 106](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/functions/function-kinds.tex#L106).

## recursive definition; exponentiation; isomorphic; structuralist → definisi rekursif; eksponensiasi; isomorfis; strukturalis

OLP-0052 fixes the recursive clauses for addition, multiplication and exponentiation over an arbitrary Dedekind algebra; OLP-0053 compares such algebras through isomorphism and reports the structuralist use of that result. The international stems are recognizable adapted scholarly borrowings. The canon supports their orthography, academic prose frame, number and arithmetic components only; the exact formulas and arguments control the specialist meanings, and no native Javanese foundations attestation is claimed.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0009, larik 80](../translation/content/sets-functions-relations/sets/pairs-and-products.tex#L80).

## propositional logic; propositional variable; propositional or logical connective → logika proposisional; variabel proposisional; konektif proposisional utawa logis

Logika is directly attested in scholarly Javanese and the official orthographic table supports logika and logis. Proposisional, variabel proposisional and konektif remain provisional adapted technical expressions. OLP-0057 states their roles and preserves all five connective symbols; OLP-0058 supplies the exact formation clauses. OLP-0071 applies Aturan Proposisional to the complete LK propositional-rule section while the displayed formulas and left/right rule labels fix the specialist scope, so no canon passage is treated as formal-logic authority. OLP-0100 applies Aturan Proposisional to the complete tableau connective-rule section while the displays fix every polarity. OLP-0107 revalidates konektif proposisional as the class containing the conjunction, disjunction and conditional operators treated by nine tableaux.

Logika is directly attested and the canon supports controlled borrowing and academic exposition, but none of the five complete connective names is attested as a mathematical-logic term. OLP-0058 preserves each symbol, formation clause and primitive-basis abbreviation exactly; OLP-0061 supplies the truth clauses. OLP-0071 uses the connective symbols themselves in the four rule headings and preserves every negation, conjunction, disjunction and conditional left/right rule, so those formal roles do not depend on unattested lexical inference. OLP-0104 preserves negation, conjunction and the material conditional through all three quantified worked tableaux and nine exercises. OLP-0107 preserves panyelakan, panggandhengan, pamisahan and implikasi through their exact signed expansion rules and derivability claims. OLP-0114 preserves all 14 propositional axiom schemas for conjunction, disjunction, conditional, negation, truth and falsity with their exact labels.

Semantik is directly attested in sustained Javanese scholarly linguistics, and the orthographic canon supports controlled adaptation of foreign stems. Sintaksis and the complete formal compounds are not attested there, so they remain explicitly provisional. The inductive formation clauses and exact string-equality definition in OLP-0058 control the technical meanings. OLP-0112 states the chapter's editorial convention that every connective and quantifier is primitive except the defined biconditional.

## truth-functional; truth value; valuation; evaluation function → fungsional tumrap kabeneran; nilai kabeneran; valuasi; fungsi evaluasi

The canon directly supplies general truth vocabulary, fungsi in nonmathematical and scholarly contexts, semantik, academic exposition and controlled borrowing. These roles support only the components and register. OLP-0057 restricts truth functionality to dependence on component truth values and excludes modal, epistemic, temporal and many-valued questions. OLP-0061 stipulates the two-value set, defines valuations as functions from propositional variables to it, and recursively fixes the evaluation of constants, atoms and every active connective. The four formal expressions remain provisional rather than canon-attested. OLP-0099 pairs exactly one nilai kabeneran with one sentence in each signed formula and preserves both True and False signs.

## satisfaction; satisfiable; unsatisfiable → relasi nyukupi; bisa dicukupi dening valuasi; ora bisa dicukupi dening valuasi

Nyukupi has only an ordinary fulfill-or-meet reading in the checked language evidence; no model-theoretic attestation is claimed. OLP-0057 states the technical relation with the valuation as subject and the formula as object. OLP-0061 labels it transparently as relasi nyukupi, defines every recursive clause, and proves equivalence with evaluation value True; panyukupan remains only a provisional descriptive noun in earlier overview prose. The specialization is source-defined, explicit and reversible. OLP-0095 quantifies over structures or valuations satisfying every open assumption and preserves every positive and negative satisfaction polarity. OLP-0097 preserves satisfaction of the combined assumption sets, both premise formulas and the identity-elimination conclusion. OLP-0105 places panyukupan on the semantic side of its opening contrast, opposed explicitly to definitions through the existence of closed tableaux. OLP-0109 defines satisfaction for both signs, sets, branches and whole tableaux, preserving every positive and negative semantic polarity. OLP-0111 proves that reflexive identity and both signed identity replacements preserve satisfaction of a tableau branch.

## tautology; semantic entailment or consequence; contingent → tautologi; akibat semantis; kontingen

The canon supports scholarly logika and semantik, general truth vocabulary, academic prose and controlled borrowing; it does not attest these complete formal notions. OLP-0057 distinguishes their quantifier patterns. OLP-0062 defines tautology, entailment, satisfiability and contingency through exact valuation quantifiers and the satisfaction relation; its explanatory gloss says that from Gamma, A is an akibat semantis, avoiding the causal verb ngakibatake. The adapted labels and descriptive consequence phrase remain provisional and source-controlled. OLP-0091 retains akibat for entailment in the list of semantic notions, while the following clauses distinguish it from the provability relation. OLP-0095 proves semantic entailment from natural-deduction derivability and keeps that relation distinct in every induction case. OLP-0105 retains akibat for entailment in the list of semantic notions, while the following definitions distinguish it from tableau provability. OLP-0109 derives semantic entailment from tableau provability and keeps the finite signed premise witness distinct from the final model condition.

## formation sequence; unique readability; parsing → urutan pambentukan; mung bisa diwaca kanthi siji cara; uraian

Use transparent descriptive phrases in academic prose. OLP-0057 and OLP-0059 replace the awkward bisa kawaca kanthi unik with mung bisa diwaca kanthi siji cara and describe parsing as one structural decomposition. OLP-0060 defines an urutan pambentukan as a finite language-string sequence ending in the target formula whose entries are atomic or built from strictly earlier entries, then proves its equivalence with inductive formula generation by a prefix lemma and strong induction. The complete formation and parsing vocabulary remains provisional rather than canon-attested.

## uniform substitution; local determination; semantic deduction theorem → substitusi seragam; penentuan lokal; teorema deduksi semantis

Retain controlled adapted technical compounds under the borrowing policy. OLP-0059 defines substitusi seragam with every occurrence, replacement site and simultaneous variable-to-formula pairing explicit. OLP-0061 defines local determination by agreement on exactly the variables occurring in A and concludes equality of the two evaluations of A. OLP-0062 fixes the semantic deduction theorem by the exact equivalence between Gamma entailing A material-implies B and Gamma union A entailing B. The notation and source clauses, rather than the canon, remain the formal authority.

## derivation system; axiom; inference rule; derivable → sistem derivasi; aksioma; aturan inferensi; bisa diderivasi

OLP-0063 titles the shared first-order/propositional wrapper and gives its reuse guidance. OLP-0064 defines a derivation as a finite syntactic object and an axiomatic derivation by fixed axioms or inference rules. Retain derivasi and the adapted proof-theory vocabulary under the borrowing policy; canon does not attest the complete compounds. OLP-0099 states that every non-assumption signed formula results from an inference rule applied to an earlier formula on the same branch. OLP-0103 applies the signed connective inference rules step by step, preserving each source line, result line and rule label. OLP-0104 records every quantified and propositional inference rule beside its exact source line and resulting signed formula. OLP-0107 uses bisa diderivasi for conjunction projections and pairing, disjunction injections, modus ponens and two conditional claims. OLP-0108 preserves a closed tableau for A(c), its converted universal witness and the two quantified instance derivability relations. OLP-0110 adds three exact identity inference rules to the tableau system and keeps both prerequisite formulas explicit for each signed replacement. OLP-0111 checks the reflexive and true identity rules explicitly and identifies the false-signed identity case as the exact dual. OLP-0116 follows each derivable target through its exact axiom instances and modus-ponens steps, including the Gamma-relative chain example. OLP-0117 uses the derivable predicate for each exact chained implication and then applies the quantified rule to the final universally quantified target. ## soundness; completeness; validity → soundness (jaminan semantis); kelengkapan; validitas

OLP-0064 fixes soundness as derivability guaranteeing entailment or validity and completeness as the converse. Retain the source term soundness with the descriptive gloss jaminan semantis so it cannot collapse into ordinary validity; OLP-0068 consequently renders a sound and complete system as one that ngemu jaminan semantis lan jangkep rather than reusing sah. OLP-0072 spells out the same soundness condition directly as the system deriving only valid sequents, again avoiding ordinary sah. Kelengkapan and validitas remain recognizable provisional adaptations because canon does not attest the metatheoretic terms. OLP-0091 states that soundness and completeness establish the mutual correspondence between the semantic and proof-theoretic notions, replacing a bare-sameness wording while preserving the theorem names. OLP-0095 states soundness as derivability from open assumptions implying semantic entailment and derives validity and consistency consequences. OLP-0105 states that soundness and completeness establish the mutual correspondence between semantic and tableau proof-theoretic notions, using cocog siji lan sijine rather than literal identity. OLP-0109 states tableau soundness as closed tableau implying unsatisfiability and derives validity, semantic consequence and consistency in that direction. OLP-0111 extends tableau soundness to identity by proving that no satisfiable closed tableau can result from any identity-rule expansion. OLP-0118 states that soundness and completeness together establish the correspondence between semantic and proof-theoretic notions.

## consistency; inconsistent; syntactic counterpart → konsistensi; ora konsisten; padanan sintaktis

OLP-0064 defines inconsistency by arbitrary derivability and its semantic counterpart. OLP-0065 uses an empty-right sequent derivation, OLP-0066 derives falsity, and OLP-0067 uses a closed tableau from true-signed premises. The system-specific criteria and their arbitrary-derivability consequences remain source-controlled; canon does not attest the proof-theory compound. OLP-0091 defines ora konsisten exactly by derivability of falsum and defines consistency through the explicit outer negation of that property. OLP-0092 gives four exact relations between provability and consistency, including both complementary inconsistent extensions and explicit membership of not-A. OLP-0093 states the inconsistency of a disjunction with the negations of both disjuncts and realizes the claim by deriving falsum. OLP-0095 proves by contrapositive that satisfiability implies consistency, using derivability of falsum as the exact inconsistency criterion. OLP-0105 defines ora konsisten by a finite all-true premise set with a closed tableau and reuses that exact criterion in explosion and compactness. OLP-0106 applies ora konsisten to four exact tableau claims: contraction, the two provability equivalences, explicit inconsistency and exhaustive cases. OLP-0107 states that a disjunction with the negations of both disjuncts is ora konsisten and supplies the exact closed tableau witness. OLP-0109 proves by contrapositive that satisfiability implies consistency, using a finite closed-tableau witness for inconsistency. OLP-0118 defines axiomatic consistency through non-derivability of false and records the equivalent explosion characterization.

## natural deduction; introduction and elimination rules; proof by cases; indirect proof; conditional proof → deduksi natural; aturan introduksi lan eliminasi; bukti miturut kasus; bukti ora langsung; bukti kondisional

OLP-0066 ties proof by cases to both disjuncts, indirect proof to contradiction from the negated conclusion, and conditional proof to consequence from the antecedent, and each introduction or elimination label to its displayed rule. OLP-0085 defines the system's assumption start, paired introduction/elimination rules and sentence-tree derivations while describing its purpose as modeling informal mathematical reasoning. OLP-0086 verifies every propositional introduction and elimination direction, including disjunction case analysis and the distinction between negation introduction and classical falsehood. ## strict linear order; group; Peano arithmetic and induction schema; pure set, urelement and naive comprehension; mereology, parthood, proper part and mereological sum → urutan linear ketat; grup; aritmetika Peano lan skema induksi; himpunan murni, urelemen lan skema komprehensi naif; mereologi, relasi dadi perangan saka, perangan sejati lan jumlah mereologis

OLP-0170 presents five distinct examples. The strict-order axioms assert irreflexivity, total comparability and transitivity; group axioms specify right identity, associativity and a right inverse; Peano arithmetic includes successor, addition, multiplication, a definition of order and the induction schema. The pure-set example distinguishes empty set and urelements and derives unsatisfiability from naive comprehension using self-nonmembership. ## nonstandard structure versus nonstandard arithmetic model; standard and nonstandard elements; finite satisfiability; enumerable nonstandard true-arithmetic model → struktur nonstandar lan model aritmetika nonstandar; unsur standar lan nonstandar; kapenuhan subhimpunan winates; model aritmetika sejati nonstandar kang bisa didhaptar

## weak arithmetic theory; explicit nonstandard Q-model; successor, addition, multiplication and order interpretations; unprovability from countermodel → teori aritmetika ringkih; model Q nonstandar kang ditemtokake kanthi cetha; tafsir penerus, panjumlahan, pingan lan urutan; ora bisa dibuktekake saka model kang mbantah

OLP-0195 contrasts true arithmetic and PA with weaker Q. Model K extends the naturals by a fixed successor point a, defines addition and multiplication by explicit tables, and uses an order relation including a<a; it satisfies Q while falsifying irreflexivity. ## nonstandard PA model; discrete strict order; predecessor; standard and nonstandard blocks; dense block order; countable reduct isomorphism → model PA nonstandar; urutan ketat diskret; pradésesor; blok standar lan nonstandar; urutan blok rapet; isomorfisme redukt model kang bisa didhaptar

OLP-0196 uses PA-provable conditions to make the model order strict, linear and discrete. Every nonzero element has one predecessor, while zero has none; a nonstandard element generates a two-sided successor block. Distinct blocks are disjoint and ordered uniformly, with no first nonstandard block, no last block in a nonstandard model, and a dense order among nonstandard blocks. Only for enumerable models is that dense endpoint-free quotient denumerable and isomorphic to the rational order; the conclusion concerns the reduct to order and equality, not full arithmetic isomorphism. OLP-0248 proves that a decidable index set defined by a class C of partial computable functions forces C to be empty or the whole class. ## size of sets; enumeration; countability/uncountability → ukuran himpunan; enumerasi; katetungan/ora katetungan

Teges matematis manut konteks definisi utawa panganggoné ing Open Logic Project. Katrangan basa mènèhi landhesan kanggo ragam utawa ejaan.

## denumerable; at most denumerable → bisa dietung lan tanpa wates; ukurane ora ngluwihi himpunan kang bisa dietung lan tanpa wates

Kabeh 39 panganggone dibandhingake karo paragraf sumber Inggris. OLP-0193 nyambungake bijeksi karo Nat, OLP-0339 nyebut aleph_0, lan OLP-0137 mbedakake domain winates saka domain denumerable. Enumerable manut OLP-0029 uga kalebu dhaptar winates lan dhaptar kosong; ora ateges komputabel. Kanon mung ndhukung register, ejaan, lan tembung cacah umum.

Panggonan ing sumber Jawa [OLP-0058, larik 21](../translation/content/propositional-logic/syntax-and-semantics/formulas.tex#L21).

## approach (methodological route in a proof outline) → ancangan

Ancangan nyata digunakake ing andharan metodhe panliten basa Jawa ing JV-P040. Ing OLP-0128, tembung iki nyebut dalan kanggo mbuktekake teorema, dudu teorema utawa aturan inferensi anyar. Transfer menyang konteks bukti isih minangka pilihan panyunting kang bisa dibalekake; ora ana atestasi istilah teknis modern kang diklaim.

Panggonan liyane kacathet ing [tabel CSV](DECISION_OCCURRENCES.csv) lan [data JSON](DECISIONS.json).
